\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{(2^{\lambda}-a+1)}}\underbrace{\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{(2^{\lambda}-a+1)}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0}^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w^{\prime}\in\left(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S^{\prime}})\right)\\
y_{1}^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0}^{\prime},w^{\prime},y_{1}^{\prime},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S^{\prime}}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},y_{0}^{\prime}||\overrightarrow{y}||y_{1}^{\prime},w_{1},w^{\prime})\})\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}}_{\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle}

Finally, to understand
\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}
, we expanding the projector
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}
:

\displaystyle\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}=

\displaystyle\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},w,y_{1},\overline{S}\\
\overline{S}\text{ is good}\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle\!\langle y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rvert_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},w,y_{1},\overline{S}\\
\overline{S}\text{ is good}\end{subarray}}\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle\!\langle y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rvert_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},w,y_{1}\\
X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1},\overline{S^{\prime}}\\
w\in\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S^{\prime}})\end{subarray}}\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S^{\prime}}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},y_{0}||\overrightarrow{y}||y_{1},w_{1},w)\})\rangle

\displaystyle\times\langle y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S^{\prime}}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},y_{0}||\overrightarrow{y}||y_{1},w_{1},w)\})\rvert_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},w,y_{1}\\
X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1},\overline{S^{\prime}}\\
w\in\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S^{\prime}})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{(2^{\lambda}-a+1)}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle\langle y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S^{\prime}}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},y_{0}||\overrightarrow{y}||y_{1},w_{1},w)\})\rvert_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1},\overline{S^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle\langle\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rvert_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

Hence,

\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}=\sum_{\overline{S^{\prime}},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle\!\langle\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rvert

∎

Proof of
Lemma

6.12
.

Fix some
y_{0},w,y_{1},\overline{S}
where
\overline{S}
is good and
a=\mathsf{count}(\overline{S})
and
b=\mathsf{len}(\overline{S})
. We start by looking at what

\lvert\psi_{y_{0},w,y_{1},\overline{S}}\rangle=\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\lvert y_{0}\rangle_{\mathsf{A}}\lvert w\rangle_{\mathsf{B}}\lvert y_{1}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}\lvert\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle_{\mathsf{\overline{ST}}},

Then we have the following:

\displaystyle\lvert\psi_{y_{0},w,y_{1},\overline{S}}\rangle=

\displaystyle\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\lvert y_{0}\rangle_{\mathsf{A}}\lvert w\rangle_{\mathsf{B}}\lvert y_{1}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}\lvert\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle_{\mathsf{\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2^{an}\cdot\Pi_{i=1}^{b}(2^{\lambda}-i+1)}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Z\in\{0,1\}^{an}\\
R\in\left(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\right)^{b}_{\mathrm{dist}}\end{subarray}}\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\lvert y_{0}\rangle_{\mathsf{A}}\lvert w\rangle_{\mathsf{B}}\lvert y_{1}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}\lvert\mathbb{G}\left(\overline{S},R,Z\right)\rangle_{\mathsf{\overline{ST}}}

Notice that the above is zero if there’s no line in
\mathbb{G}\left(\overline{S},R,Z\right)
of the form
({\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w,\overrightarrow{r},z)
for some
\overrightarrow{x},w_{1},\overrightarrow{r},z
. That is that
\overline{S}=\overline{S^{\prime}}\cup\{({\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w)\}
.Then the above looks like:

\displaystyle\lvert\psi_{y_{0},w,y_{1},\overline{S}}\rangle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2^{an}\cdot\Pi_{i=1}^{b}(2^{\lambda}-i+1)}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Z^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{(a-1)n}\\
R^{\prime}\in\left(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}})\cup\{w\}\right)^{b-2}_{\mathrm{dist}}\\
z\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
\overrightarrow{r}\in\left(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus R^{\prime}\right)^{2}_{\mathrm{dist}}\end{subarray}}\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\lvert y_{0}\rangle_{\mathsf{A}}\lvert w\rangle_{\mathsf{B}}\lvert y_{1}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}

\displaystyle\times\lvert\mathbb{G}\left(\overline{S^{\prime}},R^{\prime},Z^{\prime}\right)\cup\{({\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w,\overrightarrow{r},z)\}\rangle_{\mathsf{\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{2n}(2^{\lambda}-a+1)\sqrt{2^{an}\cdot\Pi_{i=1}^{b}(2^{\lambda}-i+1)}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Z^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{(a-1)n}\\
R^{\prime}\in\left(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\right)^{b-2}_{\mathrm{dist}}\\
z\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
\overrightarrow{r}\in\left(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus R^{\prime}\right)^{2}_{\mathrm{dist}}\\
y_{0}^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w^{\prime}\in\left(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S^{\prime}})\right)\\
y_{1}^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0}^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{A}}\lvert w^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{B}}\lvert y_{1}^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{A}}

\displaystyle\times\lvert\mathbb{G}\left(\overline{S^{\prime}},R^{\prime},Z^{\prime}\right)\cup\{({\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},(y^{\prime}_{0},y_{1}^{\prime}),w_{1},w^{\prime},\overrightarrow{r},z)\}\rangle_{\mathsf{\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{2n}(2^{\lambda}-a+1)\sqrt{2^{an}\cdot\Pi_{i=1}^{b}(2^{\lambda}-i+1)}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0}^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w^{\prime}\in\left(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S^{\prime}})\right)\\
y_{1}^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
Z\in\{0,1\}^{an}\\
R\in\left(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\right)^{b}_{\mathrm{dist}}\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0}^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{A}}\lvert w^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{B}}\lvert y_{1}^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}

\displaystyle\times\lvert\mathbb{G}\left(\overline{S^{\prime}}\cup\{({\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},(y_{0}^{\prime},y_{1}^{\prime}),w_{1},w^{\prime})\},R,Z\right)\rangle_{\mathsf{\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{(2^{\lambda}-a+1)}}\underbrace{\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{(2^{\lambda}-a+1)}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0}^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w^{\prime}\in\left(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S^{\prime}})\right)\\
y_{1}^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0}^{\prime},w^{\prime},y_{1}^{\prime},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S^{\prime}}\cup\{({\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},(y_{0}^{\prime},y_{1}^{\prime}),w_{1},w^{\prime})\})\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}}_{\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},3}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},\overrightarrow{x},w_{1}}\rangle}

Finally, to understand
\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}
, we expanding the projector
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}
:

\displaystyle\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}=

\displaystyle\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},w,y_{1},\overline{S}\\
\overline{S}\text{ is good}\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle\!\langle y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rvert_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},w,y_{1},\overline{S}\\
\overline{S}\text{ is good}\end{subarray}}\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle\!\langle y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rvert_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},w,y_{1}\\
\overrightarrow{x},w_{1},\overline{S^{\prime}}\\
w\in\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S^{\prime}})\end{subarray}}\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S^{\prime}}\cup\{({\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w)\})\rangle

\displaystyle\times\langle y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S^{\prime}}\cup\{({\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w)\})\rvert_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},w,y_{1}\\
\overrightarrow{x},w_{1},\overline{S^{\prime}}\\
w\in\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S^{\prime}})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{(2^{\lambda}-a+1)}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},3}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},\overrightarrow{x},w_{1}}\rangle\langle y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S^{\prime}}\cup\{({\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w)\})\rvert_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\overrightarrow{x},w_{1},\overline{S^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},3}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},\overrightarrow{x},w_{1}}\rangle\langle\chi^{\mathfrak{l},3}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},\overrightarrow{x},w_{1}}\rvert_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

Hence,

\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}=\sum_{\overline{S^{\prime}},\overrightarrow{x},w_{1}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},3}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},\overrightarrow{x},w_{1}}\rangle\!\langle\chi^{\mathfrak{l},3}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},\overrightarrow{x},w_{1}}\rvert

∎

Appendix D
Proofs of
Section

6.6

Proof of
Lemma

6.13
.

Recall that we want to analyse:

\lvert\phi\rangle=V^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}_{R}V^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}_{R}V^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}_{R}\lvert x_{0}\rangle\lvert w_{1}\rangle\lvert x_{1}\rangle\lvert\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle

In the below calculation, we don’t explicitly write the normalisation, and can be verified.

\displaystyle\lvert\phi\rangle

\displaystyle=V^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}_{R}V^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}_{R}V^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}_{R}\lvert x_{0}\rangle\lvert w_{1}\rangle\lvert x_{1}\rangle\lvert\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Z\in\{0,1\}^{an}\\
R\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}})^{b}_{\mathrm{dist}}\end{subarray}}V^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}_{R}V^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}_{R}V^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}_{R}\lvert x_{0}\rangle\lvert w_{1}\rangle\lvert x_{1}\rangle\lvert\mathbb{G}(\overline{S},R,Z)\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Z\in\{0,1\}^{an}\\
R\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}})^{b}_{\mathrm{dist}}\\
(r_{1},r_{2})\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus R)^{2}_{\mathrm{dist}}\\
z\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
y_{0},y_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w_{2}\in\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S})\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0}\rangle\lvert w_{2}\rangle\lvert y_{1}\rangle\lvert\mathbb{G}(\overline{S},R,Z)\cup\mathfrak{p}({\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}},(x_{0},x_{1}),(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w_{2},(r_{1},r_{2}),z)\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Z\cup\{z\}\in\{0,1\}^{(a+1)n}\\
R\cup\{(r_{1},r_{2})\}\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}})^{b_{2}}_{\mathrm{dist}}\\
y_{0},y_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w_{2}\in\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S})\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0}\rangle\lvert w_{2}\rangle\lvert y_{1}\rangle\lvert\mathbb{G}(\overline{S}\cup\{({\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}},(x_{0},x_{1}),(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w_{2})\},R\cup\{(r_{1},r_{2})\},Z\cup\{z\})\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},y_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w_{2}\in\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S})\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0}\rangle\lvert w_{2}\rangle\lvert y_{1}\rangle\lvert\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S}\cup\{({\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}},(x_{0},x_{1}),(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w_{2})\})\rangle

\displaystyle=\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},3}_{\overline{S},(x_{0},x_{1}),w_{1}}\rangle

Hence, we have

\displaystyle V^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}_{R}V^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}_{R}V^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}_{R}\lvert x_{0}\rangle\lvert w_{1}\rangle\lvert x_{1}\rangle\lvert\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle=\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},3}_{\overline{S},(x_{0},x_{1}),w_{1}}\rangle

∎

Proof of
Lemma

6.14
.

Recall that we want to analyse:

\lvert\phi\rangle=V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{r},1}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{AB\overline{ST}}}\lvert x^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}

In the below calculation, we don’t explicitly write the normalisation, and can be verified.

\displaystyle\lvert\phi\rangle

\displaystyle=V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{r},1}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{AB\overline{ST}}}\lvert x^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}

\displaystyle=V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w_{2}\in\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S})\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w_{2},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S}\cup\{(XL,\overrightarrow{x},y_{0}||\overrightarrow{y},w_{1},w_{2})\})\rangle_{\mathsf{AB\overline{ST}}}\lvert x^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}

\displaystyle=V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w_{2}\in\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S})\\
Z\in\{0,1\}^{an}\\
R\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}})^{b}_{\mathrm{dist}}\\
z\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
\overrightarrow{r}\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus R)^{a^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{dist}}\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w_{2},\mathbb{G}(\overline{S}\cup\{(XL,\overrightarrow{x},y_{0}||\overrightarrow{y},w_{1},w_{2})\},R\cup\{\overrightarrow{r}\},Z\cup\{z\})\rangle_{\mathsf{AB\overline{ST}}}\lvert x^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w_{2}\in\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S})\\
y_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
Z\in\{0,1\}^{an}\\
R\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}})^{b}_{\mathrm{dist}}\\
z\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
\overrightarrow{r}\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus R)^{a^{\prime}}_{\mathrm{dist}}\\
r\in\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus R\cup\{\overrightarrow{r}\}\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w_{2},y_{1}\mathbb{G}(\overline{S}\cup\{(XR,\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},y_{0}||\overrightarrow{y}||y_{1},w_{1},w_{2})\},R\cup\{\overrightarrow{r}||r\},Z\cup\{z\})\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w_{2}\in\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S})\\
y_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w_{2},y_{1}\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S}\cup\{(XR,\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},y_{0}||\overrightarrow{y}||y_{1},w_{1},w_{2})\})\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

Hence, we have

\displaystyle V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{r},1}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{AB\overline{ST}}}\lvert x^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}=\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

∎

Proof of
Lemma

6.15
.

Recall that:

\displaystyle W^{\mathsf{glued}}=

\displaystyle\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},321}\right)\cdot V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot V_{L}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot\left(I-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},32}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},321}\right)\cdot V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},3}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},32}\right)\cdot V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\cdot V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(I-\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},3}\right)\cdot V_{R}^{(3),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\cdot V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\cdot V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\right)

We know
\lvert\phi\rangle=\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\lvert\phi\rangle
. Then we also have the following:

\displaystyle\left(I-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}\right)\lvert\phi\rangle=

\displaystyle\left(I-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\lvert\phi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\left(I-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}\right)\lvert\phi\rangle

Similarly, we have:

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left(I-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}\right)\lvert\phi\rangle}_{\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle}=

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\left(I-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}\right)\lvert\phi\rangle

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}\right)\lvert\phi\rangle}_{\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle}=

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}\right)\lvert\phi\rangle

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\right)\lvert\phi\rangle}_{\lvert\phi_{3}\rangle}=

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\right)\lvert\phi\rangle

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\right)\lvert\phi\rangle}_{\lvert\phi_{4}\rangle}=

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},123}\right)\lvert\phi\rangle

And we have
\lvert\phi\rangle=\sum_{i\in[4]}\lvert\phi_{i}\rangle
.
Then for
i\in[4]
, we compute that
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}W^{\mathsf{glued}}\lvert\phi_{i}\rangle-W^{\mathsf{glued}}\lvert\phi_{i}\rangle
.

We start by computing the above for
\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle
.

\displaystyle W^{\mathsf{glued}}\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle

\displaystyle=W^{\mathsf{glued}}\left(I-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}\right)\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle

\displaystyle=\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},321}\right)\cdot V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot V_{L}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}\left(I-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}\right)\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle

Notice that
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}
commutes with
\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},321}
. Also, since
\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle
is in the subspace
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}
, we can write it as a superposition over
\lvert x_{0}\rangle\lvert w_{1}\rangle\lvert x_{1}\rangle\lvert\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle
. Then by
Lemma

6.13
, we get that
V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot V_{L}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}
on
\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle
is in
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}
. Hence, we get
(I-\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}})W^{\mathsf{glued}}\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle=0
.

Next, we compute the above for
\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle
.

\displaystyle W^{\mathsf{glued}}\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=W^{\mathsf{glued}}\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}\right)\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},32}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},321}\right)\cdot V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},12}\right)\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle

Notice that
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}
commutes with
\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},32}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},321}
. Also, since
\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle
is in the subspace
\Pi^{{\mathcal{R}},1}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}
, by
Lemma

6.10
, we can write it as a superposition over
\lvert\chi^{1}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle\lvert x^{\prime}\rangle
. Then by
Lemma

6.14
, we get that
V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}\cdot V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}
on
\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle
is in
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}
. Hence, we get
(I-\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}})W^{\mathsf{glued}}\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle=0
.

Similarly by, we have for
i\in\{3,4\}
,
(I-\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}})W^{\mathsf{glued}}\lvert\phi_{i}\rangle=0
. Hence, we get
(I-\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}})W^{\mathsf{glued}}\lvert\phi\rangle=0
.
∎

Appendix E
Proofs of
Section

7.4

Lemma E.1
.

We have the following:

\|\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}^{\dagger}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi_{\leq t}-\left(\sum_{\overline{S^{\prime}},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle\!\langle\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rvert\right)\Pi_{\leq t}\|_{\mathrm{op}}=O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

The proof of the above is similar to
Lemma

6.10
.

Notice that
Lemma

6.11
, we have

\sum_{\overline{S^{\prime}},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle\!\langle\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S^{\prime}},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rvert=\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{cR,12}.

Notice that

\Pi^{cR,12}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}=\Pi^{cR,12}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}=0,

\Pi^{cR,12}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},3}=\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},3}

and

\Pi^{cR,12}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},4}=\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},4}

Then we can see how
\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},i}
behaves with the above projector. In particular, we have the following:

Lemma E.2
.

We have the following:

\|\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}^{\dagger}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}\Pi_{\leq t}\|_{\mathrm{op}}=O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

Lemma E.3
.

We have the following:

\|\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}^{\dagger}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}\Pi_{\leq t}\|_{\mathrm{op}}=O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

Lemma E.4
.

We have the following:

\|\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}^{\dagger}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},3}\Pi_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},3}\Pi_{\leq t}\|_{\mathrm{op}}=O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

Lemma E.5
.

We have the following:

\|\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}^{\dagger}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},4}\Pi_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},4}\Pi_{\leq t}\|_{\mathrm{op}}=O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

Proof of
Lemma

7.9
.

We recall that we want to estimate:

\displaystyle\gamma=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}W^{\mathsf{glued}}-W^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}W^{\mathsf{glued}}-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle+\left\|W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle+\left\|W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}+O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}+O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

Where the second line is by triangle inequality, and the third line is by
Lemma

E.2
, and the fourth line is true because
\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}=\left(I-V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\right)
and
\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}=\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}
. Next, we compute

\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}.

We know that the subspace represented by
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}
is spanned by
\lvert x_{0},w_{1},x_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle
for
x_{0},x_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{n}
,
w_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}
and
\overline{S}
is some good state parameter. Let
a=\mathsf{count}(\overline{S})
and
b=\mathsf{len}(\overline{S})
.

Computing
\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}\lvert x_{0},w_{1},x_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle
:

We start by computing:

\displaystyle\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}\lvert x_{0},w_{1},x_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},3}_{\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S}),(x_{0},x_{1})w_{1}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{2^{{\lambda}}-a}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{1},y_{0}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S}\cup\{({\mathcal{L}}{\mathcal{L}},(x_{0},x_{1}),(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w)\})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{2^{{\lambda}}-a}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{1},y_{0}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{F}(\overline{S}\cup\{({\mathcal{L}}{\mathcal{L}},(x_{0},x_{1}),(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w)\})\rangle

Where the second line is by
Lemma

6.13
.

Computing
W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\lvert x_{0},w_{1},x_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle

We start by computing:

\displaystyle\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle=

\displaystyle W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\lvert x_{0},w_{1},x_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\lvert x_{0},w_{1},x_{1},\mathfrak{F}(\overline{S})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2^{(b-a)n}\left((2^{{\lambda}}-a)\ldots(2^{{\lambda}}-b+1)\right)}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{\mathcal{U}}\in\{0,1\}^{(b-a)n}\\
{\mathcal{V}}\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))^{b-a}_{\mathrm{dist}}\end{subarray}}\lvert x_{0},w_{1},x_{1},\mathbb{F}(\overline{S},{\mathcal{U}},{\mathcal{V}})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2^{(b-a)n}\left((2^{{\lambda}}-a)\ldots(2^{{\lambda}}-b+1)\right)}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{\mathcal{U}}\in\{0,1\}^{(b-a)n}\\
{\mathcal{V}}\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))^{b-a}_{\mathrm{dist}}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{1},y_{0}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus(\imaginary(\overline{S})\cup{\mathcal{V}}))\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{2^{{\lambda}}-b}}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathbb{F}(\overline{S},{\mathcal{U}},{\mathcal{V}})\cup(\{(x_{0}||w_{1}||x_{1},y_{0}||w||y_{1})\},)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{2^{(b-a)n}\left((2^{{\lambda}}-a)\ldots(2^{{\lambda}}-b)\right)}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{1},y_{0}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus(\imaginary(\overline{S})))\\
{\mathcal{U}}\in\{0,1\}^{(b-a)n}\\
{\mathcal{V}}\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S})\cup\{w\})^{b-a}_{\mathrm{dist}}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle\times\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathbb{F}(\overline{S}\cup\{({\mathcal{L}}{\mathcal{L}},(x_{0},x_{1}),(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w)\},{\mathcal{U}},{\mathcal{V}})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{2^{{\lambda}}-a}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{1},y_{0}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{F}(\overline{S}\cup\{({\mathcal{L}}{\mathcal{L}},(x_{0},x_{1}),(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w)\})\rangle

Hence, we have
\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle=\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle
. Since the subspace represented by
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}
is spanned by
\lvert x_{0},w_{1},x_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle
, hence

\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(1),\mathsf{mid}}-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}=0.

Finally, we get

\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}W^{\mathsf{glued\text{-}fwd}}-W^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},1}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}=O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

∎

Proof of
Lemma

7.10
.

We recall that we want to estimate:

\displaystyle\gamma=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}W^{\mathsf{glued\text{-}fwd}}-W^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}W^{\mathsf{glued\text{-}fwd}}-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle+\left\|W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle+\left\|W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},32}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},321}\right)V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle+O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

Where the second line is by triangle inequality, and the third line is by
Lemma

E.3
. Also notice that
\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},321}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}=0
.
Hence, we have

\displaystyle\gamma=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}+O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

Next, we compute

\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

We know by
Lemma

6.10
that the subspace represented by
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}
is spanned by
\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},1}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{AB\overline{ST}}}\lvert x^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}
s.

Computing
\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},1}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{AB\overline{ST}}}\lvert x^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}
:

We start by computing:

\displaystyle\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\left(\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},1}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{AB\overline{ST}}}\lvert x^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{r},2}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{r},2}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{2^{{\lambda}}-a}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},y_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S})})\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},y_{0}||\overrightarrow{y}||y_{1},w_{1},w)\})\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{2^{{\lambda}}-a}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},y_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S})})\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{F}(\overline{S}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},y_{0}||\overrightarrow{y}||y_{1},w_{1},w)\})\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

Where the above is by
Lemma

6.14
.

Computing
W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},1}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{AB\overline{ST}}}\lvert x^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}

We start by computing:

\displaystyle\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle=

\displaystyle W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},1}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{AB\overline{ST}}}\lvert x^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
v\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))\end{subarray}}\lvert u,v,x^{\prime},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y}||u,w_{1},v)\})\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
v\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))\end{subarray}}\lvert u,v,x^{\prime},\mathfrak{F}(\overline{S}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y}||u,w_{1},v)\})\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{2^{{\lambda}}-a}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},y_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S})})\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{F}(\overline{S}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},y_{0}||\overrightarrow{y}||y_{1},w_{1},w)\})\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

Hence, we have
\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle=\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle
. Since the subspace represented by
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}
is spanned by
\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},1}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{AB\overline{ST}}}\lvert x^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}
, hence

\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{L}^{(2),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}=0.

Finally, we get

\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}W^{\mathsf{glued\text{-}fwd}}-W^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}=O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

∎

Proof of
Lemma

7.11
.

We recall that we want to estimate:

\displaystyle\gamma=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}W^{\mathsf{glued\text{-}fwd}}-W^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},3}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}W^{\mathsf{glued\text{-}fwd}}-(I-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger})W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},3}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle+\left\|W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}(I-W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger})\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},3}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},3}-\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},32}\right)V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}-(I-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger})W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},3}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle+O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},3}\right)V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}-W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},3}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}}_{\gamma_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},32}\right)V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}-(W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger})W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},3}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}}_{\gamma_{2}}

\displaystyle+O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

Where the second line is by triangle inequality, and the third line is by
Lemma

E.4
, and the last line is by triangle inequality. Next, we compute
\gamma_{1}
and
\gamma_{2}
. Before computing this, we know by
Lemmas

6.11
and
6.12
that the subspace represented by
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},2}
is spanned by
\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}
s where
\mathsf{len}(\overrightarrow{x})>2
.

Computing
\gamma_{1}
:

We start by looking at some fixed
\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}
with
a=\mathsf{count}{S}
and
b=\mathsf{len}(\overline{S})\cup\mathsf{len}(\overrightarrow{x})
.
Then we will show

\Big(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}-W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Big)\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}=0

We start by computing the first term (call it
\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle
):

\displaystyle\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{L}^{(3),\mathsf{mid}}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{r},1}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{AB\overline{ST}}}\lvert x^{\prime}\rangle_{\mathsf{C}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2^{n}(2^{{\lambda}}-a)}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))\end{subarray}}\lvert y,w,x^{\prime},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{L}},\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1},w)\})\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2^{n}(2^{{\lambda}}-a)}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))\end{subarray}}\lvert y,w,x^{\prime},\mathfrak{F}(\overline{S}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{L}},\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1},w)\})\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

Where the above is by
Lemma

6.14
. Next, we compute the second term (call it
\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle
):

\displaystyle\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle=

\displaystyle W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},2}_{\overline{S},X,\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{2^{{\lambda}}-a}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},y_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1},w)\})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{2^{{\lambda}}-a}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},y_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{F}(\overline{S}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{R}},\overrightarrow{x}||x^{\prime},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1},w)\})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2^{n}(2^{{\lambda}}-a)}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}^{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))\end{subarray}}\lvert y,w,x^{\prime},\mathfrak{F}(\overline{S}\cup\{(X{\mathcal{L}},\overrightarrow{x},\overrightarrow{y},w_{1},w)\})\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

Hence
\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle=\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle
. Hence, we get
\gamma_{1}=0
.

Similarly, we can show that
\gamma_{2}=O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})
.
Hence combining, we get

\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}W^{\mathsf{glued\text{-}fwd}}-W^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},3}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}=O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}}).

∎

Proof of
Lemma

7.12
.

We recall that we want to estimate:

\displaystyle\gamma=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}W^{\mathsf{glued\text{-}fwd}}-W^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},4}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}W^{\mathsf{glued\text{-}fwd}}-(I-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger})W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},4}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle+\left\|W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}(I-W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger})\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},4}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\left(I-\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},3}\right)V_{R}^{(3),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}-(I-W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger})W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},4}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle+O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{R}^{(3),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}-W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},4}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}}_{\gamma_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\left(\Pi^{{\mathcal{L}},3}\right)V_{R}^{(3),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}-(W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}W_{L}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger})W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},4}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}}_{\gamma_{2}}

\displaystyle+O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})

Where the second line is by triangle inequality, and the third line is by
Lemma

E.5
, and the last line is by triangle inequality. Next, we compute
\gamma_{1}
and
\gamma_{2}
. Before computing this, we know by
Lemma

6.12
that the subspace represented by
\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},4}
is spanned by
\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},3}_{\overline{S},(x_{0},x_{1}),w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}
s.

Computing
\gamma_{1}
:

We start by looking at some fixed
\overline{S},(x_{0},x_{1}),w_{1}
with
a=\mathsf{count}{S}
and
b=\mathsf{len}(\overline{S})
.
Then we will show

\Big(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{R}^{(3),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}-W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Big)\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},3}_{\overline{S},(x_{0},x_{1}),w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}=0

We start by computing the first term (call it
\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle
):

\displaystyle\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}V_{R}^{(3),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(2),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}V_{R}^{(1),\mathsf{mid},\dagger}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},3}_{\overline{S},(x_{0},x_{1}),w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\lvert x_{0},w_{1},x_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lvert x_{0},w_{1},x_{1},\mathfrak{F}(\overline{S})\rangle

Where the above is by
Lemma

6.13
. Next, we compute the second term (call it
\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle
):

\displaystyle\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle=

\displaystyle W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\lvert\chi^{\mathfrak{l},3}_{\overline{S},(x_{0},x_{1}),w_{1}}\rangle_{\mathsf{ABC\overline{ST}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{2^{{\lambda}}-a}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},y_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{G}(\overline{S}\cup\{({\mathcal{L}}{\mathcal{L}},(x_{0},x_{1}),(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w)\})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle W_{R}^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda}),\dagger}\frac{1}{2^{n}\sqrt{2^{{\lambda}}-a}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y_{0},y_{1}\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
w\in(\{0,1\}{{\lambda}}\setminus\imaginary(\overline{S}))\end{subarray}}\lvert y_{0},w,y_{1},\mathfrak{F}(\overline{S}\cup\{({\mathcal{L}}{\mathcal{L}},(x_{0},x_{1}),(y_{0},y_{1}),w_{1},w)\})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lvert x_{0},w_{1},x_{1},\mathfrak{F}(\overline{S})\rangle

Hence
\lvert\phi_{1}\rangle=\lvert\phi_{2}\rangle
. Hence, we get
\gamma_{1}=0
.

Similarly, we can show that
\gamma_{2}=O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}})
.

Hence combining, we get

\left\|\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}W^{\mathsf{glued\text{-}fwd}}-W^{\mathfrak{m}({\lambda})}\mathcal{O}_{\mathsf{comp}}\right)\Pi^{\mathsf{Good}}\Pi^{\mathfrak{l},4}\Pi_{\leq t}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}=O(t^{2}/2^{{\lambda}}).

∎
